  • Comparable-sales analysis is directional and based on public listing/sold snippets available during this search; buyer should verify MLS sheets, disclosures, plot plans, road noise, sump/easement status, and garage count with the buyer agent before touring or offering.
  • Comparable-sales confidence is limited because public listing pages often omit garage spaces, lot size, condition, concessions, and exact neighborhood adjustments for sold comps.
  • Comparable-sales valuation is an estimate from public listing data, not an appraisal or title/inspection review.

  • Listings with no verified garage space were excluded from both matches and close_hits, even when otherwise close: examples found included 70 Muller Rd, 13 Hilltop Dr, 15 Marrett Rd, 38 Paulson Dr, 60 Harriett Ave, and 141 Wilmington Rd.

  • Several MLS mirror pages conflict on status for older listings; where a recent Redfin/Trulia/Realtor-derived page showed contingent, pending, sold, or active-under-contract, I kept the property out of matches.
  • Several potentially interesting active homes in Wakefield, North Reading, Wilmington, and Lynnfield were excluded because no garage was verified or because status was contingent/pending/sold/off-market.
  • Several public pages showed stale or conflicting status/prices; where newer sources showed sold, contingent, pending, or no garage, the property was excluded or treated only as a comp.
  • Some public real-estate mirrors lag MLS status. I gave priority to pages crawled today or yesterday and to exact property pages when available.
  • Statuses change quickly. I prioritized pages crawled or checked closest to September 12, 2026 and exact-address listing pages where available.
  • Sump pump and easement findings are based only on public listing disclosures visible in searched sources. Unknown does not mean clear.
  • Sump pump and easement status are not reliably proven by silence in consumer listings; they are marked unknown unless a listing explicitly disclosed the condition or, for 7 Hillcrest, explicitly showed no basement.
  • Sump pump and easement status are only marked verified_clear when an affirmative source supported that; otherwise they remain unknown. Most consumer listing pages do not reliably disclose easements or sump status.
  • Sump pump and easement status generally could not be verified from public consumer listings; unknown does not mean clear.
  • Sump pump and easement status were left unknown unless a source explicitly identified a sump pump or another clear issue; absence from consumer listing text was not treated as proof.
  • Sump pump and easement status were left unknown unless explicit listing language proved otherwise; absence from a consumer listing was not treated as proof.
  • Sump pump and easement statuses are marked unknown unless a listing explicitly disclosed the item. Absence of a sump/easement mention was not treated as verified clear.
  • Sump pump and easement statuses require MLS disclosures, seller statements, inspection, and title review; I marked them unknown unless a listing source explicitly stated a sump pump or easement.
  • Sump-pump and easement status were not treated as clear unless a source affirmatively verified them; most consumer listing pages do not address those items.
  • The second-pass exact-address sweep found newer Lynnfield listings including 11 Homestead Rd and 58 Grove St that were not fully represented in older summary results.
  • The town list contains 12 towns, although the instruction says all 11 towns; I treated all 12 listed towns as in scope.
  • Valuations are rough public-source comp estimates, not appraisals. MLS status and disclosures should be confirmed directly with a buyer agent before relying on any property.
  • "UNKNOWN" for sump/easement does not mean the house is clear. Verify seller disclosures, deed/plot plan, and MLS details before relying on it.
  • Road-noise judgments from web research are screening tools only. Driving the area at rush hour is still worthwhile.
